# Maintenance Reminders — design package MR_V2_2 (2026-10-06): full reading notes

Package: `build/maintenance-reminder-v2/sources/design-MR_V2_2-2026-10-06/`
Read 2026-10-06. Authority for comparison: Chunk 1 spec `CONFLUENCE-886931488-Chunk1-MR-2026-10-06.md`
(Confluence page last modified 2026-10-05 19:58; design cross-check stated as 2 Oct 2026) and Chunk 2 spec
`CONFLUENCE-897679389-Chunk2-MR-2026-10-06.md` (same date). The specification is later than every screenshot
and every note in this package; **where they differ, the spec wins**. Screenshots are the designer's
reference captures (mostly of the *current* ShopView app or of earlier board states), not requirements.

Conventions: quoted text is verbatim from the image/file. "OLDER THAN SPEC" = the package shows something the
current spec contradicts (spec wins). "DESIGN-ONLY" = a tester-visible detail the spec does not mention.
Story tags: S1–S9, S13, S14, S21 (Chunk 1); S10–S12, S16–S19, S22 (Chunk 2).

Layout: A screenshots · B Markdown · C handoff HTML/JSON · D `_tools` · **X "Older than spec — spec wins" (222 items)** · **Y "Design-only details a tester would see" (66 items)** · **Z "Reading coverage" table (144 files, all read 100%)**.

(4) "Open | No work order, None, —" vs S13-R14 "A row with no work order shows a dash". (5) "template" as vocabulary and OQ-12a starter templates vs S1-R3 "There is no modal and no template". (6) "A user-set snooze date stays exact" vs spec Skip (S9-R13) with no snooze. (7) Permissions row "Edit reminder wording" vs S19-N7 no wording editor and S1-R1 no reminder settings. (8) "Send a reminder | more than view, OQ-13 unresolved" — resolved in spec S14-R10 (edit-customer permission; hidden). (9) OQ-09 "last day of the expiry month" — resolved in spec S8-R10 (End date as a day). (10) OQ-10 confidence thresholds — resolved in spec S11-R24 (locked 1 Oct). ### B3. design_handoff_maintenance_reminders/02-asset-dashboard.md (18,448 B) — read every line
- **Dated:** "Aligned to spec v12, 23 Sep 2026", plus addendum "Spec v15 alignment (24 Sep)". Older than the 5 Oct spec.
- Tester-observable statements: meter cards show "Last recorded" (value, date, source WO) and "Current estimate" (Estimated badge, rate, source, three-segment confidence bar); confidence colours Low orange / Medium blue / High green; recorded reading carries no grade; source line e.g. "346,050 mileage · measured from 4 visits · last read 6 days ago"; rate "640 a week"; one flat list, Schedule a column; columns Service (badge) · Schedule · Interval · Due · ⋮; status exactly Overdue / Due today / Due soon; Due soon begins at first reminder; no badge before; due rule wording "Based on mileage estimate · Based on engine hours estimate · Based on the certificate term · Based on the calendar"; no number on overdue rows; Due cell one line, other candidates under row menu "Other triggers" (24 Sep); button "Enter mileage" (24 Sep); reading dialog CURRENT | NEW, "Last recorded 29 Aug 2026 · WO S3780-15211", WO not a link, no "WHAT THIS CHANGES"; reading on a WO shows "In the shop" until invoiced; no Pause (24 Sep); "Skip" on compliance row menu (24 Sep); asset search by unit number, customer unit number or VIN.
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (spec wins):** (1) Row menu "Mark complete · Snooze · Create estimate" (routine) vs S9-R9 "Mark complete, Create work order, Skip, and Other triggers"; compliance "Snooze absent" vs S9-R11 compliance offers Skip. (2) Snooze "moves the due date" vs S9-R13 "Skip moves no date and records no completion". (3) Schedule header menu "Pause · Remove" (corrected 24 Sep to "Remove") vs S6-R13 "Remove from schedule" with confirm "Remove 402 from Highway Tractor PM? …". (4) Mark complete on a work order "resets from that work order's invoice date" vs S18-R6 "Mark complete resets the service at once from its Reset date … It never waits for an invoice". (5) Completed elsewhere "Date + location, kept. Readings optional" vs S18-R4 "asks optionally for the shop's name and a reading. Its Reset date has no default and is required". (6) Compliance record "Effective date · Expiry date … pick the last day of that month" and OQ-09 last-day assumption vs S8-R2/S8-R10 "Start date, End date … End date lands on the same day number". (7) Record form titled "Add history record" vs spec "+ Add record" (S7-R6, S8-R13). (8) "Type (type-ahead)" on the record vs S8-R2 "type, term, Start date, End date, optional certificate number, one optional attachment" (type as named on the service). (9) "The term is mandatory, so the date always resolves … Sep 2027 / Based on the 12-month term · no certificate on file" vs S3-N2/S8-N2 "A compliance service with no matching record … cannot come due, reads No record, and is excluded from every count". (10) "Compliance and term-based rows print Aug 2026, not 31 Aug 2026; Due today holds for the whole month" vs S11-R13 "14 Oct 2026 · Certificate" and S12-R4 due today on the End date. (11) "Soon is future only. At low confidence a date still ahead reads Soon" vs S11-R9 "Every estimated due date shows a month, at every confidence" (Soon is email-only, S19-R9). (12) Enrolment badge "Not enough data, per meter" vs S7-R15 "Needs mileage reading or Needs engine hours reading". (13) Enrolment order "schedule → both current readings → expected running rate → last-service date" (header) vs S7-R2 (the file itself records their removal on 16 Sep). (14) "A live Comes due column resolves per service as the advisor types (R-23)" vs the file's own removal note and S7-R2. (15) Bulk: "Units already on another schedule are shown and not selectable" vs S7-R25 "an asset already on the chosen schedule is greyed … reads Already on this schedule"; "Last-service dates are set per unit afterwards" vs S7-R26 dates taken from each asset's own history. (16) Certificates tab descoped — consistent with S8-N3; but records reachable "from the compliance service that owns them" only vs S8-R13 Compliance section on the asset card. (17) "Telematics marked LATER" vs S10-N5 (none in v1). (18) Confidence "numbers … being reconciled" — locked in spec S11-R24 (1 Oct).
- **Consistent with spec:** three badges (S9-R7); Due soon at first reminder (S9-R8); no Active/Paused (S9-N1); one row however many cycles (S9-R10); Due cell one line + Other triggers (S9-R12); no number on overdue (S11-R13); rate without unit (S11-R18); reading dialog current left/new right (S10-R2); no forecast (S10-N4); In the shop (S10-R11); nothing refused (S10-N1); consent checkbox writing the customer's field (S7-R13/R18); bulk from customer's asset list (S7-R24); blank last-service date counts from today (S7-R4).
- **DESIGN-ONLY:** confidence colours (Low orange, Medium blue, High green — spec only says Low never red, S9-R17); the "Last recorded … · WO …" line where "the work order is not a link"; asset search also by "customer unit number".
- Designer's gaps 2.1–2.15 (validation screens cut; undo toast/correction undrawn; Pause vs Paused; re-enrolling same schedule; same-named services on two schedules; Comes due one vs all candidates; WO completed never invoiced; consent switched mid-enrolment; archived schedule/removed service; future last-service date; snooze past next cycle; mark complete earlier than last completion; 40+ services; reading permission; bulk result state) and 10 test questions. Several are now answered by the spec (e.g. 2.4 by S7-E8, 2.5 by S9-E1, 2.10 by S7-R4 "cannot be in the future", 2.7 by S18-R6/S13-R26).

### B5. design_handoff_maintenance_reminders/04-algorithms.md (14,825 B) — read every line
- Chunk 04, logic review (7 documentation boards). Body ~16 Sep ("Revised 16 September 2026"); addendum "25 Sep corrections". Thresholds explicitly "⚠ provisional", OQ-10/OQ-09/OQ-21 open. Older than the spec, which locked the confidence table on 1 Oct and replaced the certificate rule on 2 Oct.
- Tester-observable statements: reading = fact with timestamp and author; recording re-evaluates thresholds immediately; correction is a new reading, original kept; two things record a reading (a user changing mileage on a WO → "In the shop"; invoicing fixes it); nothing refused; per-meter independent rates; no borrowed rate; No data = separate state, calendar governs; recorded reading carries no grade; provenance line "≈ 346,050 mileage · measured from 4 visits · last read 6 days ago"; computed dates show a month; whichever trigger first; compliance with no record cannot come due; missed service appears once; Active/Paused do not exist. 25 Sep corrections: no money in panel/hover/add confirmation, rows "4 lines · 2.1 hours", parts keep part numbers only; engine hours "hours" in full; V4 panel "PM-A · Overdue"; W13 compliance due "Due Sep 2026"; W14 status uses real WO statuses (Estimate, In progress, Complete).
- Worked fixture (V-35): asset 402, 342,417 mileage on 29 Aug 2026 (WO S3780-15211), rate 640 a week, today 9 Sep 2026; estimate "≈ 346,050 mileage · measured from 4 visits · last read 6 days ago · High"; PM-A every 15,000, last 18 Jun 2026 → due at 346,000 → Sep 2026; enter 346,900 → rate 710 a week; PM-A due today (from 15 Sep); PM-D overdue by 4,500 mileage (from 1,400); engine hours unchanged.
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (spec wins):** (1) Rate "Take the two most recent recorded readings" vs S11-R2 "what the meter added across the unit's last three usable pairs, divided by the days those pairs span". (2) Confidence thresholds "not set — OQ-10" vs S11-R24 locked table (1 Oct). (3) Status "Upcoming" at first reminder vs S9-R7 "due soon". (4) "Snooze moves the due date" vs S9-R13 Skip (moves no date). (5) Calendar "at = the next occurrence of month M" vs S2-R7 day-and-month. (6) Compliance "last day of the expiry month" vs S8-R10/S12-R4 End date as a day. (7) "Start reminders … months only, 1–4" vs S3-R8 Remind before expiry. (8) "A larger service absorbs a smaller one when they land together" / "supersession" vs S2-E6/S12-R10 covering is stated by the shop, never inferred. (9) "A service completed on a work order does not reset until that work order is invoiced (R-42)" — still true for added lines (S18-R6), but Mark complete now resets at once (S18-R6/R1); the file has no Mark-complete-at-once path. (10) Fixture "PM-D overdue by 4,500 mileage" vs S11-R13 "carries no figure at any confidence". (11) Fixture "PM-A becomes due today" from an estimate — consistent with spec, but conflicts with the 25 Sep worklist note (B4) that an estimate never reads Due today. (12) Cascade lists "DOLLAR TOTALS" on the worklist and an "Email queue" vs S4-R6 / S5-N1. (13) WO card "due today · odometer updated" vs S10-R9 Mileage wording. (14) W13 "Due Sep 2026" compliance month vs S11-R13 "14 Oct 2026 · Certificate". (15) W14 status list "Estimate, In progress, Complete" — consistent with S13-R25 "as the app names it" (spec adds "Review included").
- **Consistent with spec:** S10-R4/R5/R10/R11, S11-R10, S11-R14, S11-R17, S11-E1, S11-R18, S11-R12 (rounding not stated here), S9-R10, S16-R16 summary form.
- Designer's gaps 4.1–4.14 (OQ-10, OQ-09, bundling window, rate edge cases, sync vs optimistic cascade, timezone of "today", decay vs step at 90 days, one-reading asset, rounding, rate cap, candidates per cell, tile pricing, invoice as reset+recording order/void, Low covering two cases) and 11 test questions. Several are now answered by the spec: 4.4 (S10-E4 same-day takes higher, S11-R5 non-increasing discarded, S11-R20 >1 year discarded), 4.6 (S12-R14 location day), 4.10 (S11-R19 cap 1,500 a day / 24 hours a day), 4.13 (S12-E5 reading recorded first, then reset; S18-E2 reversal).

### B6. design_handoff_maintenance_reminders/05-auto-email.md (6,602 B) — read every line
- **Dated:** "Rewritten 16 September 2026" (three shop-worded emails → one hardcoded email; Reminder settings screen deleted). Entire chunk assumes **automatic daily sending**, which the spec deferred to v2 on 2 Oct.

Subject "Preventive maintenance coming up"; header shop logo, name, phone; greeting "Hi {customer},"; opening "You have some preventive maintenance we want to remind you about."; table unit · service · due with state on the row ("Past due · Due today · Coming up"); closing "Give us a call and we will find a time that suits your schedule."; sign-off shop name and phone; footer why received. Deleted list (three emails, wording editor, Default/Edited/Reset to default, per-email toggles, Send a test, Sending sub-tab, singular/plural lines, unsubscribe). Consent moved to 07.
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (spec wins):** (1) "One consolidated email per customer. A 50-unit fleet gets one email" vs S19-R1/S14-R6 one asset per send. (2) "the daily job", "consent off means the daily job skips that customer" vs S5-N1/S19-R1 "Nothing sends it automatically in v1" (deferred block). (3) Greeting "Hi {customer}," vs S19-R19 "first and last name of the person it goes to … Hi Dave Brabay … Hello". (4) Sign-off "shop name and phone" vs S19-R19 "Best regards, the user's name, the organization's name and the telephone of the location chosen in the header". (5) "Only what is reasonably near (R-61). Nothing due eleven months out." vs S19-R6 "overdue, due today and due within the next 91 days" and S19-R7 next two upcoming as Coming up when none. (6) Header with shop logo — not in spec. (7) Contact-card "Resend" with confirm vs S14-R5. (8) "Soon … whether Medium also renders Soon is unstated" — spec S19-R9 "including every Low confidence date … Where a row's date would be a guess".
- **Consistent with spec:** fixed wording (S19-R2), any-mix opening line (S19-R2), states on the row Past due / Due today / Coming up (S19-R8), Soon (S19-R9), no unsubscribe (S19-R15), WO-email mechanism (S19-N8).
- **DESIGN-ONLY:** subject "Preventive maintenance coming up", opening line, closing line (spec gives none of these words; spec S19-R2/R19 only constrain them).
- Gaps 5.1–5.9 and 7 test questions (OQ-01–04; consent-off confirmation/count; no-email customer; Soon vs Medium; 40-row email; read receipts; asset naming in customer copy).

- Content: product context (Vue 3 + Quasar 2; references Technician Efficiency report, Inspection Templates, WO list row pills, `PageSearchInput` + `FilterBar`; light and dark themes); 16-screen inventory (Settings programs list/editor; Work Orders → Maintenance tab with Due and In-progress zones; tab count badge; enrolment dialog; asset panel; WO asset-card section; completion enrolment nudge; reminder delivery settings; shop digest; batched customer email; tokenised booking page; customer maintenance card; Reports → Maintenance read-only alias; deleted front-of-app banner and Plans tab); per-screen jobs, content and states (programs bound to asset classes; one meter per program; mandatory calendar backstop; tiers nest/absorb; compliance tier with 12-month term and 1-month buffer, never snoozed; blast-radius "this moves 40 assets"; zone rows with Send/Call/Create estimate; "Needs a current reading" never blank; low-confidence estimates; count badge; enrolment dialog with program/current reading/last service, live threshold preview "reads 217,649 → A at 227,649 · B at 242,649 · C at 267,649", compliance requires current certificate date, "No suitable program? Create one" / "Request a program"; implausible-reading state (2004 Ford F-350, 217,649 miles, 11 engine hours) "this reading doesn't look right, confirm or replace it"; asset panel actions complete/pause/snooze/create WO/edit/activity log/delete; WO section grouped by program with totals and "Not priced"; digest each morning; batched customer email with booking windows; booking page "the shop will confirm"); cross-cutting verbatim copy "Needs a current reading", "Not priced", "No customer record"; data realities (asset type on ~3 in 10 assets; stale readings; compliance vs convenience); light/dark themes; `data-test-id` on every interactive element; wide tables scroll in their own container.
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (spec wins) — essentially all of it:** worklist under Work Orders with count badge (S13-R1/N1); programs bound to asset classes, one meter per program, tiers, absorbing (S1-R6, S2-R5, S2-E6); blast-radius confirmation (S6-R3); money totals/value (S4-R6/S16-N5); Send/Call/booking windows/self-booking page (S14, S19); digest (S19-N5); reminder delivery settings with wording editor and merge fields (S1-R1, S19-N7); enrolment collecting the current reading and a live threshold preview (S7-R2); compliance requiring a certificate date in enrolment (S7-R6 "No record with + Add record"; S8-R11); implausible reading "rejects" (S10-N1); panel actions pause/snooze/edit/delete (S9-N1/N2/R13, S1-E1); "Needs a current reading" (S7-R15/S16-R12 "Needs mileage reading"/"Needs engine hours reading"); WO section grouped by program with prices (S16-R16, S16-R3); Reports → Maintenance alias (not in spec).
- **DESIGN-ONLY (spec silent, tester could see):** "No customer record" where a customer name would go and the record is missing (spec S13-E5 removes rows whose customer is deleted — no such label); light and dark themes; wide tables scroll inside their own container. (Asset type is populated on ~3 in 10 assets — data fact relevant to test data.)
- Consistent with spec: panel collapsed and neutral (S16-R1); compliance never snoozed past deadline → now Skip (differs); dedupe duplicate canned lines across schedules (S17-R8/S12-R12); blank last service = today, said in the dialog (S7-R4 "Blank means counting starts today, stated in the modal").

### B22. uploads/maintenance-reminders-design.md (34,090 B, 308 lines) — read every line (two passes: 1–200, 200–308)
- **Dated:** "Design agreed 2026-08-27 … Reference build origin/SV-3780-maintenance-reminders @ cbe5bf0c, draft PR #2786, QA at sv3780.qa.shopview.com"; author Milos Vasic. The oldest design decision record; wholly superseded by the 5 Oct spec.
- Content: why (POC exists: 6 tables, 28 endpoints, ~50 FE files); problem; industry grounding (meter by asset class; tiers nest PM-A…PM-D; compliance 49 CFR §396.17 / CVIP NSC Standard 11, schedule at 11 months; duty-cycle multiplier); decisions D1–D14 (advisor as primary user; one row per visit; projection with four-tier fallback + calendar backstop; `mileage_reported_at`; worklist as fifth Work Orders tab with count badge behind a feature flag; WO permission atoms; delete `MaintenanceDueBanner.vue`, no bell; three surfaces; program owns meter/tiers/intervals/lines/warning/email; ad-hoc schedules removed; two-zone tab; one schedule row per tier; blast-radius on interval change; customer click = request); object model (MaintenanceProgram, ProgramTier routine/compliance, AssetEnrolment; reused maintenance_schedule, work_order statuses `estimate, in_progress, approved, declined, ready_for_review, complete, invoiced, paid`; CannedLine timeEstimate/techTime); surfaces and permissions (routes `administration/maintenance-programs`, `reports/maintenance-reminders`); due timing (sources incl. imported WOs, DVI snapshots; fallback ladder ≥2 points / 1 point default rate / 0 points gap in units / "Needs a current reading"; implausible readings rejected); flows A–E; failure modes 1–9; notification matrix, digest, cadence (routine 3 touches; compliance 60/30/14/7 then daily; one email per recipient per day, batched); customer self-booking; corrections F1–F13 against the POC branch; deleted/kept/new; open questions; sequencing; out of scope (telematics, SMS, appointments).
- Facts about the **existing (pre-feature) app** a tester may still meet (from F-items/§13): legacy asset Maintenance tab with "Add Schedule" and "Start From A Plan (Optional)" (matches screenshot A1); legacy Reports → Maintenance with "Reminders" and "Plans" tabs; a 7-option "What Makes It Due" dropdown; a Miles/Kilometers selector; an "Only this location" toggle; `MaintenanceDueBanner.vue` front-of-app banner; WO status set `estimate, in_progress, approved, declined, ready_for_review, complete, invoiced, paid` (relevant to S13-R25 "every value up to invoicing, Review included"). The spec's S1-R1/S9-R16 describe the new placements; the spec does not say what happens to these legacy surfaces (except PRD OQ-07 deleting the banner) — a tester-visible gap, noted here as **design-only/legacy**.
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (spec wins):** worklist under Work Orders with count badge and feature flag (S13-R1/N1; spec: "ships to every organization at release, with no feature flag"); programs bound to vehicle types with one meter and tiers that absorb (S1-R6, S2-R5, S2-E6); blast-radius re-anchoring of enrolled assets (S6-R2/R3); enrolment live threshold preview and required certificate date (S7-R2/R6); default rate per asset class and gap-in-units (S11-R14, S11-R13); implausible readings rejected / non-monotonic points rejected (S10-N1/N3); "Needs a current reading" (S7-R15/S16-R12); digest, automatic batched customer email, cadence, booking page (S5-N1, S19-N5, S19-R1); "Request a program" (not in spec); Snooze (S9-R13); permissions on WO atoms for the worklist (S13 prerequisite "view customers").

### B23. uploads/reading estimation spec.md (11,277 B) — read every line
- **Dated:** "SV-3780 · 2026-09-04". Superseded by Chunk 2 S10/S11 (rate rules locked 1 Oct).
- Content: §1 the drawn card numbers are wrong (342,000 → 348,200 in 6 days = 1,033 mi/day etc.; plausible values 342,200–343,700); §2 one formula `estimate = last_known_reading + rate × days_elapsed`; §3 four rate sources strongest first: 3.1 measured (pairs from WOs, imported WOs, cycles, DVI; weighted average with linear weights 1,2,3…; guards: discard Δ ≤ 0, discard intervals < 7 days, cap long intervals (not required), never cache incrementally; floor recommendation: one interval = two visits with the count on the label), 3.2 customer's stated weekly average, 3.3 regional default by running type (show a month: "Probably due around October · national average for long-haul, we've seen this unit once"), 3.4 telematics = recorded; §4 three behaviours (Known exact, Projected ≈ rounded, Unknown no number); §5 replace "as of today" with "last read N days ago"; §6 field never expires; per-service Fresh/Stale/Expired, expired → needs-a-reading; cap the projection beyond one interval; §7 rounding: recorded exact; projections nearest 100 under 30 days, nearest 500 beyond, none beyond one interval; §8 engine hours own rate, plausibility rejection (2004 F-350, 217,649 mi / 11 hrs); §9 lower reading confirmed never refused, recompute all rates and thresholds incl. queued reminders; §10 open decisions.
- **Consistent with spec (survived into S10/S11):** discard non-increasing pairs (S11-R5); discard pairs < 7 days apart (S11-R4); never cache the rate, recompute from stored pairs (S11-R7, S10-E3); one usable pair (two visits) is the floor (S11-E2, S11-R23); imported history and WOs both used (S11-R3); engine hours independent, never derived from miles (S11-R10); recorded shown exactly (S11-R12); provenance "last read N days ago" (S9 cards / handoff); a lower value saves (S10-N3); telematics not in v1 (S10-N5).
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (spec wins):** weighted average over all pairs vs S11-R2 "last three usable pairs … added together and read as one period"; customer's stated average and regional/running-type defaults vs S11-R14 "measured from the unit's own readings and from nothing else"; "Probably due around October …" vs S11-R9 month with confidence; rounding coarsening to 500 vs S11-R12 "nearest 100 … hours to the nearest 10"; Fresh/Stale/Expired per service and projection cap beyond one interval vs S11-R24 table and S11-R26 (24-month window); plausibility "Hours are refused" vs S10-N1/N2 (never rejected; orange confirmation) and S11-R19 (discarded for rate only); "mi" units (S10-R9).

This is worked out from how far it ran between its last 6 visits — about 1,500 miles a week…"; Assumed; "We have no reading and nothing to estimate from. Add a reading, or tell us roughly how far this unit runs in a week."); engine hours parallel field; meter interval resolves to a date "15,000 mi · for this unit, about 15 September" with info text; worklist/asset "PM-A · due around 15 Sep · estimated from 6 visits", "due around March · estimated, we've seen this unit once"; remove the calendar backstop offer; enrolment gains required "Roughly how far does this unit run in a week?" and "What kind of running does it do?" with live "PM-A will come due around 15 September".

- **Consistent with spec:** recorded shown exactly as entered (S11-R12); estimates rounded to 100 (S11-R12, for the first 30 days); engine hours a parallel independent field (S11-R10, S10-R6); provenance "Measured from 6 visits · last read 6 days ago" (S11-R25 "Measured from N visits").

## C. design_handoff_maintenance_reminders — HTML boards and JSON

Method: visible text extracted with a Python `html.parser` script (text nodes plus `placeholder`/`title`/`aria-label`/`value`/`alt` attributes; `<script>`, `<style>`, `<svg>` and `<helmet>` skipped; the small inline `text/x-dc` logic scripts in the two shell files read separately). Every extracted line was read. `Chunk 1.dc.html` and `Chunk 2.dc.html` in the handoff folder are byte-identical to the copies at the package root (checked with `cmp`). These two boards are the design the spec says it was checked against on 2 Oct 2026, so they are the most current design text in the package.

### C1. Maintenance Reminders.dc.html (5,299 B) — index page
- Text: "SV-3780 / Maintenance Reminders / 112 artboards in two review chunks: pages 1–3, then pages 4–5." Card 1 "Chunk 1 — Schedules, asset tab and worklist": 1 Settings — maintenance schedules 40 artboards; 2 The asset — Maintenance tab 16 artboards; 3 Customers — Maintenance Reminders 20 artboards; 76 artboards, Open. Card 2 "Chunk 2 — Work order, estimate and email": 4 The work order 31 artboards; 5 Estimation and the customer email 5 artboards; 36 artboards, Open.
- Navigation board only. No tester-observable product text.

### C2. SettingsSidebar.dc.html (15,063 B) — shell component
- Text: DEV TOOLS Feature Flags · SETTINGS Settings, Staff, Roles & Permissions, Locations, Departments, Taxes · SERVICE Labor Rates, Canned Lines, Fees & Discounts, Asset Types, Inspection Templates, **Maintenance** (active/idle variants) · PARTS Pricing, Bin Locations, Categories · INTEGRATIONS QuickBooks, IBS · FINANCE Payment Methods · IMPORTS Contacts, Assets, Vendors, Inventory, Invoices. Inline logic: prop `active` enum maintenance/none; `maintActive` true for "maintenance", "programs" or "delivery".
- Bearing S1-R1: the Settings entry is labelled "Maintenance" and sits directly beneath "Inspection Templates" — consistent with spec ("Settings, Maintenance, beneath Inspection Templates").

### C3. ShopviewHeader.dc.html (2,973 B) — shell component
- Text: ShopView logo (alt "ShopView"), nav (aria-label "Primary") Work Orders · Schedule · Customers · Parts · Reports, "Search ⌘K", "Clock In", avatar "AS". Inline logic: prop `nav` enum customers/workOrders/schedule/none selects the active item.
- No feature text. Note: no count badge on Customers (consistent with S13-N1).

## D. _tools — designer working files (5 files)

### D1. _tools/inv1.txt (95,405 B) — read in full (folded at 1,800 chars per line; every line read)
- **What it is:** a text inventory of an **earlier version of the Chunk 1 board**. `##GAP <section> | <flow>` lines are the section/flow headers and `##B <id> :: <frame> | …` lines hold every text string of one frame, `|`-separated, ending at `##END`. It covers the same frames as the current Chunk 1 board: Settings (c1…c6, r3…r8, t1…t5, k02/k03, e02, e04e/e04r, v1, phone ma/mb), Asset › Maintenance (s4, m1, m2/m2e/m2c, m1u, m6, m7, p2, p3, n5, m4, x1, x1r, k2, k4, p4, cs0, phone mc/me/mg/md), and Customers › Maintenance reminders (s1, s1m2, s1m, s1mc, s1mt, s1d, s1x, s1f, s1ci, b1, b5, b1r, b1p, b4, x4a/x4b/x4c, s1b, s1c, s1dl, b1t, b1o, b1e, b3, phone mh/mf).
- **Bearing:** S1–S9, S13, S14, S21 (Chunk 1). It is an earlier snapshot, so where it differs from the current board (section C4), the current board and, above it, the spec win.
- **OLDER THAN SPEC (the spec wins in each case):**
  - k4 / md / m2c: the certificate has **"Effective Oct 2025 / Expiry Oct 2026"**, with "A certificate expires on the last day of its month." / "Expires at the end of the month" and "Expiry fills in from the month done plus the term." The spec, S8-R10, says *"Term, Start date and End date derive from one another … Both are days, picked from a calendar, so nobody has to guess whether a certificate ends on the same day or at the end of a month."* S8-R2 adds *"A record will carry type, term, Start date, End date …"*.
  - s4 / m4 / md / x1 / mf: the compliance line reads **"CVIP · AB-4471902 · expired Aug 2026" / "expires Oct 2026"**. The spec, S8-R13, gives the line as *"CVIP · AB-4471902 · ends 14 Oct 2026"*.
  - s4 / p3: **"The estimate takes the two most recent recorded readings, works out a rate …"** and "The rate is then measured again from the two most recent recorded readings." The spec, S11-R2, says *"The rate is what the meter added across the unit's last three usable pairs, divided by the days those pairs span."*
  - s4 / m1u (Due column (i)): **"At low confidence a future date reads Soon; once due, the badge carries it."** The spec, S11-R9, says *"Every estimated due date shows a month, at every confidence, with its confidence beneath it."* The m6 copy on the same inventory already says "Every estimate shows a month with its confidence", so the inventory contradicts itself here.
  - s4 confidence (i): **"Four visits inside ten months with the last reading six days old"** is graded High, and "27 a week · measured from 2 visits" is graded **Low**. The spec table, S11-R24, says *"Up to 30 days: one pair Medium, two or more High."* On that table, 2 visits (one usable pair) with a recent reading grades Medium, not Low. Note that the reading age for the engine-hours example is not stated in the inventory.
  - s4: **"Every 90,000 mileage · 500 hours · 12 months"** for PM-D. The current spec interval example is a 12-month calendar. The 90,000 here conflicts with mb's "PM-D 120,000 mileage · 36 months", and 36 months conflicts with S2-R9 *"An interval longer than 12 months is set in days"*. (The audit.json "every" entry for s4 holds the still older "Every 90,000 mileage or 500 hrs or 12 months".)
  - s4 Due cell: **"CVIP … Aug 2026 · Based on the certificate term"**, a month only. The spec, S11-R13, says *"A date from a certificate reads its End date and Certificate, for example 14 Oct 2026 · Certificate"*.
  - s4 column heading **"Interval"**, with the Due (i) using the term "comes due". p4 says "The column is called Comes due". On the worklist the spec heading is "Due status" (S13-R13). The asset-tab columns are covered under C4.
  - v1: **"AT WITHOUT A MONTH | Month | Choose a month | Choose a month."** The spec, S2-R7, says *"choosing 'At' turns the value into a (calendar) day-and-month picker, for example 15 Nov. 'At' is never a month on its own."*
  - mb compliance: the **Type** field is **"CVIP · Alberta"** with a hint. This is consistent with S3-R3 (free text). However, k4's **"Type — Typing filters the known types. Jurisdiction sits on the option row, not in the name."** describes a picker of known types. The spec, S3-R3, says *"Type is free text and required."*
  - k4: **"Term — Suggested by the type and editable. A bus CVIP runs 6 months where a tractor runs 12 …"**. The spec, S8-R10/S8-R11, derives the term from the dates and requires it; it does not say a term is suggested from the type. Flagged as older wording; S8 gives no type-based suggestion.
  - cs0: **"The edit dialog carries the same field as a checkbox in the trailing row."** The spec, S7-R18, says *"It is not in the customer edit dialog."* The current board (C4, CS0 line 1737) has dropped this sentence.
  - b5: **"Send reminder? · The friction is deliberate … / Brabay Maintenance last received this on 1 Sep 2026. / ops@brabay.example / 1 unit · PM-B overdue / Cancel · Send"**, the app's own confirmation step. The spec, S14-R4/S14-R13, says Send reminder *"opens the send email dialog of S19-R3, where the user picks who receives it from the customer's contacts"*. The spec has no separate confirm step.
  - b1t: a technician (no send permission) still sees the card with "last sent 1 Sep" and **no** Send reminder. This is consistent with S14-R10 (hidden). Reference only.
  - s1 / s1ci / s1c: tile **"Due in three months"**. The spec, S13-R2, says *"Due in 3 months"*. Column **"Status"** (s1dl): the spec, S13-R13, says *"It is headed Due status"*. Calendar row **"Calendar"** / certificate row **"Jan 2027 · Certificate"**: the spec, S11-R13, says certificate rows read the End date day, e.g. *"14 Oct 2026 · Certificate"*.
  - s1dl Status (i): **"Due today: an exact certificate or calendar date that falls today."** and Due (i) **"Certificate and calendar dates are exact."** The spec, S11-R13, says *"A date from the calendar reads its month"*. The calendar wording is covered under C4.
  - x4b / x4c: **"Greenhills Operations EVR LTD Sparwood · 22 active assets"** and "4 of 22 selected". mf has "Abode … · 22 active assets / 12 of 22 selected". Within the inventory the asset count is consistent; the spec, S7-R24/S7-R26, gives no header count. The only spec example is *"8 of 12 have a last service on record"*, which mf matches. Design-only (see below).
  - x1 / x1r / x4b / x4c consent (i): **"The customer's own setting. It covers every asset they own. Off, due dates are still tracked; only the email stops."** This is consistent in substance with S7-R14 and S7-R21. However, the spec, S7-R19, says *"Turning it off will state how many of that customer's enrolled units will not receive reminders"*, and that count does not appear here. S7-R20 says *"Every surface that shows the setting says so where it applies, with a way to add a contact"*, which also does not appear. Flagged as older (missing spec-required content).
- **Matches the spec verbatim:** m7 "Remove 402 from Highway Tractor PM? / Its maintenance history is kept. Its reminders from this schedule stop." (S6-R13). m1 / s1m2 / s1m / b3 Mark complete (i) "Resets this service now, from the date you choose. It won't wait for an invoice." (S18-R1). m6 "Skipped … Undo skip … Add record" (S9-R13). x4c "Already on this schedule" / "Enroll 3 assets" (S7-R25). x1r "Needs mileage reading" / "Needs engine hours reading" (S7-R15). mf "8 of 12 have a last service on record; the rest count from today." (S7-R26). s1ci "Needs readings" tile (S13-R2).

### D2. _tools/inv1b.txt (72,004 B) — read in full
- **What it is:** the same inventory as inv1, with repeated blocks replaced by macros «A»…«Z» that are defined at the top of the file. I expanded every macro by script and diffed the result against inv1: **0 differences**. I also read the file itself line by line. It adds no content beyond inv1, so the findings are those of D1.

- **What it is:** a more heavily compressed copy of the same inventory (macros «A»…«Z» cover more shared blocks: «H»/«F»/«I»/«J» worklist header, tiles, chip, columns; «P»/«W»/«Z»/«X» worklist rows; «U» contact card; «T» customer Assets tab; «L»/«N» phone asset header; «Q»/«B»/«S»/«V» phone chrome). Expanded by script and diffed against inv1: **0 differences**. Every line was read. Same findings as D1.

### D4. _tools/audit.json (22,395 B) — read in full (parsed; every key and value printed)
- **What it is:** the designer's **term audit**. For each board file (`Chunk 1.dc.html`, `Chunk 2.dc.html`) it maps 30 search categories to the frames where that term was found, each with up to three matched snippets of about 110 characters. The categories are: template, startFrom, price, every, lead, snooze, ce, ned, dayCert, absorb, bundl, spine, c5, dash, hrs, mi, lastmonth, today, rename, live, createLines, remindExp, needs, undo, already, enrollBtn, tip, contact, certLabel, confidence. Empty categories mean the audit found nothing: template, startFrom, lead, snooze, ce, ned, bundl, spine, c5, mi, today, rename, live and certLabel are empty for both chunks, and absorb is also empty for Chunk 2.
- **It audits an EARLIER board state than the current boards.** Chunk 1 hits include "4 lines · 3 absorbed" / "7 lines · 4 absorbed" / "absorbs PM-A when it lands" (the current board and inv1 say "covered"; the current Chunk 1 text has **0** "absorb"); "6,388 hrs", "Every 90,000 mileage or 500 hrs or 12 months" (the current Chunk 1 has **0** " hrs"); "Every 15,000 mileage or 3 months"; Chunk 2 "Create lines" on wo1/y0/w3/w4/w4o/w7/w2mr/w2mr2/w2x/y1h/y1a/y1t/y1d (the current Chunk 2 has **0** "Create lines"); and enrollBtn "Enroll in maintenance schedule" on b4/mf (the current boards say "Enroll in Schedule").
- **What a tester can take from it:** nothing new to test. Every term it lists is either already in the current boards (read in C4/C5) or superseded there. It confirms that the designer swept for the spec vocabulary (snooze, absorb, hrs, mi, price, template), and the superseded hits are logged below as older than spec:
  - "absorbed" (S2-R16 *"A service can name the other services on its schedule that it also covers"*; S2-E6 *"Covering is never inferred"*);
  - "500 hrs" / "6,388 hrs" (S2-R15 *"never hrs"*);
  - "or" between triggers (S2-R11 *"There is no or between the triggers"*);
  - "Enroll in maintenance schedule" (S7-R24 *"Enroll in Schedule"*);
  - "Create lines" on the work-order card (S16-R8 *"Add Service"*).
- **Chunk 2 "Total Hours: 3.4 hrs" / "5.2 hrs"** is the **existing work-order header** ("Total Hours: … hrs" also appears in the live-app screenshots, A-section). It is product chrome outside this feature, so it is not flagged against S2-R15, which governs how this feature names the unit. Reference only.
- Chunk 2 "every" hits worth noting, which are current: "Lines can be created only on work orders at Calgary South, where this schedule's canned lines are." (w2loc); certificates (i) "Optional. Enter any two of effective, expiry and term; the third fills itself. At least one date is needed." (i6/i7, an older wording using "effective, expiry"; the current Chunk 2 I6 says "Start date … End date", see C5). The spec S8-R10 uses *"Start date … End date"*. The email footer "Sent because Aacrest Works is the contact for these units at Staging Heavy Duty." (r1/r1p/r1t/r1u) and "There is no settings screen. The wording is fixed and identical for every shop" (r1) are consistent with S19.
- "lastmonth" i2 "Use lines closed (4 Sep)" is the switch-back link after "Use invoice date" is chosen. It is consistent with S18-R3's offered pair and is already logged as design-only under C5.

### D5. _tools/card.txt (3,998 B) — read in full
- **What it is:** a single HTML fragment, the **service hover card** for PM-A (332 px wide, design-system tokens). Its contents:
  - header "PM-A" with **"2.1 hours"** on the right;
  - "JOB DESCRIPTION": "Engine oil and filter change, chassis lubrication at 12 points, air filter inspection, brake adjustment check.";
  - "PARTS ON IT": "2× Rotella T4 15W-40, 18.9L · RT4-189", "1× Fleetguard LF9080 oil filter · LF9080", "1× Fleetguard AF25667 air filter · AF25667", "12× Chassis grease cartridge · CG-14";
  - "INSPECTION FORM": a paper-clip icon and "Tractor PM-A Inspection" in accent colour.
- **Bearing S16 (and S4-R6).** It matches S16-R7 *"Hovering a service will show what it contains: job description, parts, and where one is attached the inspection form."* This is the work-order panel's service hover. The line-count hover of S4-R5 is a different card, which lists covered services and canned lines. "PARTS ON IT" is the heading at the home location; S16-R7 names only the heading used at another location, *"PARTS CALGARY SOUTH USES (reference)"*. The heading **"2.1 hours"** is written in full, which is consistent with S2-R15 and S4-R6 (hours are the sum of the lines' hours). It shows **no price**, consistent with S4-R6.
- **DESIGN-ONLY:** the section captions "JOB DESCRIPTION", "PARTS ON IT", "INSPECTION FORM", and the part-number column on the right.

## X. Summary — Older than spec — spec wins (222 items)

Each item: [story] file — what the design shows — what the current spec says (Chunk 1 `CONFLUENCE-886931488-Chunk1-MR-2026-10-06.md`, Chunk 2 `CONFLUENCE-897679389-Chunk2-MR-2026-10-06.md`). The spec is newer than every design artefact in this package. **In every case the spec wins**, and no test case should follow the design wording listed here. Many items recur across several screenshots or documents of the same era; each distinct design-artefact/rule pairing is kept so that the file trail is complete.

## Y. Summary — Design-only details a tester would see (66 items)

Tester-visible wording or behaviour that the design shows and the spec does not mention or does not quote. These are not contradictions. A case may cite them only as design-sourced (Rule 115 ADD, cited to the design file), and where the spec later quotes different words the spec wins.

1. [S1, S13 — cross-cutting vocabulary] uploads/2026-09-02 18.14.59 / 18.15.53 / 18.38.25: the existing top-nav "Schedule" is the dispatch calendar; maintenance "schedule" is a different thing (Settings > Maintenance). Not in spec; a tester vocabulary hazard, reference only.

### Z5. Unreadable files

**None.** The coordinator's rule was applied to three screenshots that are zoomed-out canvas captures (2026-09-03 19.53.07, 2026-09-23 18.55.10, 2026-09-23 19.27.00). Methods used on them: the image viewer; Pillow crop of each region; upscale 3×–8× with LANCZOS. Their frame titles, layout and large labels were read. Their small body text is below one pixel per glyph in the capture, so no method can recover it from the image. The same frames exist as text in the html boards (`Chunk 1.dc.html`, `Chunk 2.dc.html`, Flow Map), which were read in full (C4–C6), so their content is covered.

**Outside this assignment (not read here, listed so nothing is silently dropped):** the 13 screenshots read in the earlier pass. Also the root-level boards `Maintenance Reminders Demo.dc.html`, `Canned lines per location - proposal.dc.html` and `4-work-order (old WO chrome).dc.html`, plus `_archive-pages/`, `_backup-before-ds/`, `assets/` and `support.js`. The root `Chunk 1`/`Chunk 2` boards are byte-identical to the handoff copies (checked with `cmp`). Also `_tools/` `c1.js`, `c1.tmp`, `c2.tmp`, `dsx.js`, `h.js`, `mc.js`, `mob.js`, `parts.json` and `trash.txt`, and the handoff fonts and svg (excluded by the brief).
